_source line 918 · 2026-05-24T06:09:30.628Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The worker was trying to transfer the results file from the remote server to local. It appears to have been doing an elaborate base64 encode + split + cat approach to transfer the file, and ran out of context or hit a limit before completing. The worker labeled `worker_code_empirical_humaid_analysis` (the code writer) appears to have been tasked with creating the analysis script AND running it, but got stuck trying to transfer the results back.

However, the key question is: did the analysis complete successfully on the server, even though the worker failed? The later worker `worker_exp_empirical_humaid_analysis` (status=done) appears to have succeeded and produced the same results file (`empirical_humaid_k10_results.json`) — and I already verified those results.


So the real issue was just a context limit during the file transfer itself, not the analysis or script creation. The subsequent experiment worker picked up where the code worker left off and finished the job successfully. I should verify that the analysis script was actually created on the server to confirm this theory.
```

[System 触发审查]:
论文 DDL 明天 (2026-05-25)。Round 23 审稿评分 3.5 (Borderline Conference)。

请评估以下 low-risk high-impact 修改是否能在不破坏现有论文结构的前提下提升评分：

1. MF-1 (Conjecture 1 robustness): 在正文或 limitations 中添加 1-2 句关于 Algorithm 1 在 ε<0.017 小违反下的鲁棒性讨论
2. MF-2 (Non-differential violation): 在 limitations 中更诚实地讨论 13.3% violation rate，以及 content-dependent vs outcome-dependent 的区别
3. MF-3 (Proportional effects): 添加 NLP 任务中何时 proportional effects 合理的具体例子（如 ordinal sentiment）
4. SF-3 (Missing references): 添加 Cinelli & Hazlett (2020) 对比、Carroll et al. (2006) 不适用性说明

以上修改是否足以从 3.5 推到接近 4.0？还有哪些 quick wins 我们遗漏了？

```text
1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We characterized how multi-class annotation errors interact with causal DAG topology: at $K{\geq}3$, exposure and IV can reverse treatment effect signs while five topologies preserve them. ASVs yield a topology-dependent vulnerability ranking; matrix-based corrections are provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies (FWL invariance). The practical implication is direct: identify the topology first---for four topologies, no matrix-based correction helps; for exposure, PPI++ with gold labels reduces bias by 93.6\%.
5	
6	\section*{Limitations}
7	
8	We assume non-differential misclassification (Appendix~\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}). Closed-form results (Theorems~1--2) assume linear SEMs with proportional effects ($f{=}\lambda g$); proportional effects is a reasonable approximation for ordinal classification (e.g., sentiment strength) where treatment shifts the distribution along an ordered scale, and sign preservation holds at 97.3\% under non-proportional effects (Table~\ref{tab:proportional_empirical}). FWL invariance extends to logistic models for covariate topologies (Appendix~\ref{app:fwl_proof}), but for exposure, logistic specification can increase bias (Appendix~\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}); practical recommendations do not directly extend to nonlinear models (matching, doubly-robust). The $C(\delta)$ linear interpolation assumes misclassification patterns are structurally stable as error severity varies; LLM quality changes across prompts may not follow this path. ASV computation requires estimating $B_{\mathrm{OV}}$ from gold labels via $C^{-1}$; error propagation under small gold-label budgets ($<$500) is an open question. The framework assumes a known DAG; DAG misspecification under measurement error may compound bias. General $K{\geq}3$ envelope monotonicity remains open; worst-case step deviation reaches 0.056 for exposure but empirically decreases with $K$ ($\varepsilon \propto K^{-\alpha}$, $\alpha \in [0.6, 1.5]$; Table~\ref{tab:conjecture_verify}), and Algorithm~1 routing is robust to violations below $\varepsilon{=}0.017$.
9	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_018vZizTKcPgCFXh7Fkyomg9`</summary>

```text
14	Misclassification of $A$ is non-differential with respect to outcome $Y$ and covariates $\mathbf{Z}$ if $P(A^*{=}j \mid A{=}k, Y, \mathbf{Z}) = P(A^*{=}j \mid A{=}k) = C_{jk}$ for all $j,k$.
15	\end{definition}
16	
17	In batch annotation pipelines, the LLM processes each text independently without access to $Y$, making non-differentiality an architectural invariant~\cite{ziems2024can}; Y-stratified permutation tests confirm this (1/12 rejection at $\alpha{=}0.05$; Appendix~\ref{sec:y_stratified_test}). Non-differentiality holds architecturally when $Y$ is external to the annotated text (e.g., health outcomes, voting behavior), since the LLM cannot condition on unobserved $Y$; it may be violated when $Y$ correlates with text content (e.g., topic), in which case the Y-stratified test (Appendix~\ref{sec:y_stratified_test}) provides empirical verification. Under stress testing, sign reversal occurs in 14/16 $K{\geq}3$ exposure configurations at $\varepsilon{=}0.01$ structured departure, with sign-flip rate increasing from 2.0\% to 9.67\% at $\varepsilon{=}0.20$ (Appendix~\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}); formal degradation guarantees under differential misclassification remain an open problem.
18	
19	Throughout, $\tau$ denotes the causal estimand of interest, whose definition varies by topology: the confounder's coefficient, the treatment effect, the indirect effect, or the Wald ratio (Appendix~\ref{app:confounding}ff.).
20	
21	We study seven canonical DAG topologies (Figure~\ref{fig:dag_taxonomy})~\cite{pearl2009, hernan2020causal} in which $A$ occupies structurally distinct causal roles: confounder, exposure, mediator, collider, M-bias variable, front-door mediator, and instrument. Throughout, we derive probability limits under linear structural equation models~\cite{carroll2006}, the standard framework for exact characterization of topology-dependent bias; robustness under logistic regression~\cite{stefanski_carroll1985} is verified in Appendix~\ref{sec:logistic_dgp} and~\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}.
```

</details>

_source line 1053 · 2026-05-24T10:11:30.852Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now let me assess the current state of each proposed modification:

**MF-1 (Conjecture 1 robustness)**: Already addressed in Limitations (line 8): "Algorithm~1 routing is robust to violations below ε=0.017." This was added in commit bb8c468. ✓ Already done.

**MF-2 (Non-differential violation)**: Already addressed in method.tex line 17. The current text discusses:
- Architectural invariant for batch pipelines
- Content-dependent vs outcome-dependent distinction ("Y is external to the annotated text" vs "Y correlates with text content")


- Y-stratified permutation test results and stress test performance across different violation thresholds

However, the specific 13.3% violation rate from the stress tests isn't quantified in the main text or Limitations section—it only states the assumption without showing the magnitude of violations tested.

Looking more carefully at the numbers: the 1/12 rejection rate (8.3%) is the empirical violation from the Y-stratified test, while the 14/16 configurations reflect sign reversals under structured departures. The 13.3% figure actually comes from the stress test at ε=0.0, measuring how often sign flips occur even without differential misclassification—which is different from quantifying how often the non-differential assumption itself is violated.

I think there's some confusion here about what the Director is asking for. Let me trace through what 13.3% specifically represents in the stress test results to clarify whether this is about the violation rate threshold or something else entirely. So the 13.3% violation rate is a separate metric from the sign-flip rate—that distinction between content-dependent and outcome-dependent misclassification is already covered in the text. MF-3 about proportional effects is handled, and for SF-3, both the Cinelli & Hazlett citation and the Carroll et al. explanation about why it doesn't apply to categorical misclassification are already in place.

All four reviewer items have actually been addressed in the recent commits. The real question is whether these fixes can push the paper from 3.5 to 4.0. Looking at the full picture, the paper has strong theoretical contributions—the DAG framework, the FWL invariance result, and solid empirical validation across multiple datasets and configurations. But Conjecture 1 remains unproven, which is a structural limitation that likely keeps it from reaching that 4.0 threshold. numerical evidence (545K+ curves) but not proven. Reviewers may view this as a gap in the theoretical contribution.
2. **No fully real-world end-to-end case study**: The "semi-real" study uses real confusion matrices but synthetic DGPs. A fully real causal study (real treatment, real outcome, real annotations) would be much more convincing.
3. **Proportional effects assumption**: While the 97.3% sign preservation under non-proportional effects is reassuring, the core theorems depend on this assumption. Reviewers may question how binding this is.
4. **Scope breadth vs depth**: 7 topologies is impressive in breadth but means each topology gets relatively shallow treatment in the main text.

The real bottleneck for the score is structural—the unproven conjecture and lack of a fully real case study can't be addressed in the time remaining. What I can realistically do is make sure the paper stays within the 8-page limit, all figures display properly, and there are no compilation errors.
```

  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>
